## Daily Audit — (2026-06-15 at 7:30 AM EDT)

**Tacitus integrity:** OK (with caveat) — sentinel `last_reflection_date=2026-06-15` / `last_reflection_time=2026-06-15 at 5:15 AM` aligns with notebook session #4 header at line 71. mtime ordering sensible: notebook (06-15 11:12 UTC) newer than .status.json (06-14 19:02 UTC). **Caveat:** session #4 is itself a *recovered* entry — the 5:05 AM EDT scheduled fire updated the sentinel but the notebook write did not persist; user pasted the substance back in by hand at ~7:09 AM EDT. The provenance is documented in the entry header. Yesterday's sentinel-without-content drift was resolved, but the failure mode is real and is flagged for Round 73 protocol revision.

**Lapse check:** `last_lapse_detected = null`. No new lapses today. (The 5:15 AM sentinel-content drift documented in session #4 was resolved by hand recovery, not flagged through `.status.json` — worth verifying whether the lapse-detector path should have caught this.)

**Tacitus overnight:** 2 entries in last 24h
- Session #3 (2026-06-14 at ~9:15 AM EDT) — tag: [correction] — Co-work correction of session #2's Taurine claim. Found 3 YGY Taurine SKUs the catalog missed (Rebound FX 200mg, 3.0 Rise & Restore ~300mg, Ultimate Cardio Stx 120mg); root caused to Pass 3 parser only reading `nutrients` and skipping `non_essentials` (36+ aminoacid entries affected across Arginine, Methionine, Tyrosine, Lysine, Leucine, Tryptophan). Three fixes landed: Pass 3 now parses both fields with 5% threshold; Taurine note rewritten; `dietary_with_clinical_lever` promoted to decisions.md.
- Session #4 (2026-06-15 at ~5:15 AM EDT) — tag: [engineering-idea] [recovered] — Found Label Check still routes through stale `getEffectiveCoverage()` (line 5136) using `CURRENT_COVERAGE` snapshot (line 4879, 2026-06-13 stack+diet bundle), causing (a) double-count of stack items and (b) under-count of Regimen-tab manual additions invisible to `loadRegimen()`. Periodic Table already uses `computeLiveCoverage()` (line 4046) per Round 28. Proposal: route Label Check through same pipeline; demote `CURRENT_COVERAGE` to `DIETARY_BASELINE` role only.
- Promotion candidates:
  - **Session #4 Label-Check routing proposal** → strong candidate for **decisions.md** (architectural choice unifying coverage pipelines; finishes Round 28's lap)
  - **Session #3 data-layering lesson** ("a confident-sounding note in a curated file is not the same as evidence; verify against primary source") → strong candidate for **lessons.md**

**Embed freshness:** STALE — `dashboard_integrity.py check` reports `cl-data-notebook` embed=9,974B vs source=12,840B size mismatch. Notebook 2026-06.md (06-15 11:12 U
